- **l0s-dev**: Map of the L0S operating system (a microkernel and user programs written in L0) and checklists for changing it. - **l0s-testing**: How L0S is tested and what to run when. Host unit tests (l0 test, and what calls give with no kernel), the kernel's board tests (make test-virt), the end-to-end boot cases (tests/e2e), and the screen tests that drive QEMU's keyboard, tablet and screendump (gpu-screen, keyboard, vt, widgets, procmon). - **l1l2-cache-patterns**: Two-tier caching patterns for the platform's multi-tenant microservices. L1: freecache (in-process, per service replica, tenant-keyed, 60s TTL) for GET-by-ID lookups in entity services. L2: Dragonfly (shared, Redis-compatible, tenant-keyed, 300s TTL) checked by DAL before hitting PostgreSQL. Cache invalidation via NATS events from DAL to entity services. NATS KV Store is NOT used for caching. Keywords: freecache, Dragonfly, L1 cache, L2 cache, cache invalidation, NATS events, tenant-keyed, GET-by-ID, TTL, two-tier cache, Redis-compatible. ([PremModhaOfficial/motadata-ai-pipeline](https://github.com/PremModhaOfficial/motadata-ai-pipeline/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/l1l2-cache-patterns) · ★1)

- **lab-weekly-report**: Search accessible agent chat history, verify a lab member's weekly and Gantt-chart progress, and create a natural, author-approved single-file HTML weekly report with learning reflections and optional embedded task images. - **lab-workshop**: LAB or WORKSHOP: how much checking and measuring a task gets, read from the working mode (mode_get, or sh bin/md1rd in a shell). WORKSHOP (the default) trusts the calibrations: use and confirm against the baselines, say only what falls outside range. LAB checks and measures more, treats every run as a chance to calibrate, proves it first, and writes the research before a build loop when two or more options differ in energy cost, accessibility, or fitness for the user or their teams and orgs. Use at the start of any long or costly task (a build, a test run, a release, a migration), on "lab mod… ([inputloop/vibrobeast](https://github.com/inputloop/vibrobeast/tree/HEAD/plugins/vibrobeast-skills/skills/lab-workshop) · 3 copies)
